    WHEN 1 THEN N'OrangeMoney'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'MtnMoney'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Especes'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'M2u'
    WHEN 5 THEN N'SaraMoney'
    ELSE CAST([p].[ModePaiement] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [ModePaiement], [b0].[Reference] AS [BienReference], [u0].[Nom] AS [LocataireNom]
FROM [Paiements] AS [p]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [c].[Id], [c].[BienId], [c].[LocataireId]
    FROM [Contrats] AS [c]
    WHERE [c].[EstSupprime] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [c0] ON [p].[ContratId] = [c0].[Id]
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT [b].[Id], [b].[Reference]
    FROM [Biens] AS [b]
    WHERE [b].[EstSupprime] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [b0] ON [c0].[BienId] = [b0].[Id]

    SELECT [u].[Id], [u].[Nom]
    FROM [Utilisateurs] AS [u]
    WHERE [u].[EstSupprime] = CAST(0 AS bit)
) AS [u0] ON [c0].[LocataireId] = [u0].[Id]
